It writes a header row first, then data rows, comma-separated with `\\n` line endings. Zero-match reports write their header and, where the report defines one, a zero TOTAL row.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The first row written is the report header, exactly as the report defines it.\n- Each subsequent row is written as comma-separated fields, terminated by `\\n`.\n- Line endings are `\\n` only; no `\\r\\n`.\n- A zero-match report writes its header and, where the report has a TOTAL row, a TOTAL row carrying zero values.\n- The writer does not add quoting to plain values. Values in this product are alphanumeric, numeric, or dates, and contain no commas, quotes, or newlines.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC csv-header-and-lf ===\nIntent: The CSV writer emits the header first and uses LF-only line endings.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nimport subprocess\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nlog = \"/tmp/ddkstat-csv-fixture.jsonl\"\nwith open(log, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n    f.write('{\"schema_version\":1,\"execution_id\":\"e1\",\"started_at\":\"2026-10-14T06:37:54.176Z\",\"status\":\"succeeded\",\"job\":{\"command_name\":\"build\",\"target\":\"T\",\"llm\":\"claude\",\"model\":\"haiku\"},\"result\":{\"returncode\":0,\"timed_out\":false,\"stats\":{\"cached_input_tokens\":1,\"elapsed_ms\":1000,\"input_tokens\":2,\"output_tokens\":3,\"cost_usd\":0.5,\"model\":\"haiku\"}}}\\n')\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"summary\", log)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\nassert \"\\r\" not in out, repr(out)\nlines = out.split(\"\\n\")\nassert lines[0] == \"records,skipped,succeeded,failed,first,last,input_tokens,cached_tokens,output_tokens,cost_usd,minutes\", repr(lines[0])\n=== END AC csv-header-and-lf ===\n\n=== AC csv-zero-match-total ===\nIntent: A zero-match commands report writes its header and a zero TOTAL row, and exits 0.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nimport subprocess\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nlog = \"/tmp/ddkstat-csv-empty.jsonl\"\nwith open(log, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n    f.write(\"\")\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"commands\", \"--target\", \"NoSuchTarget\", log)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\nlines = [line for line in out.split(\"\\n\") if line != \"\"]\nassert lines[0] == \"command,calls,failed,input_tokens,cached_tokens,output_tokens,minutes\", repr(lines[0])\nassert lines[-1].startswith(\"TOTAL,\"), repr(lines[-1])\n=== END AC csv-zero-match-total ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Standard library CSV-free writing only; no quoting logic is required by the sources.\n- Output goes to standard output only; diagnostics go to standard error.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-SkippedCount.md ===\n# Feature: Whole-File Skipped Count\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Counts skipped log lines over the whole file, independent of any filter. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-LogSkip.md, FEATURE-FilterTarget.md, FEATURE-FilterDate.md |\n| Provides    | whole-file skipped count |\n| Consumes    | record parser, target and date filters |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThe `skipped` column of the summary report counts skipped lines in the entire log, regardless of `--target`, `--since`, or `--until`. Filters narrow the records a report aggregates; they never change the skipped count.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The skipped count is the number of non-blank lines that the record parser rejects, computed over every line of the file.\n- The count is computed before filters are applied, so `--target` and the date options have no effect on it.\n- Blank lines are not counted (see FEATURE-LogRead.md).\n- Running the same log with and without filters yields the same skipped count.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC skipped-ignores-target ===\nIntent: The skipped count is identical with and without a --target filter.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nimport subprocess\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nlog = \"/tmp/ddkstat-skip-fixture.jsonl\"\nwith open(log, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n    f.write('{\"schema_version\":1,\"execution_id\":\"e1\",\"started_at\":\"2026-10-14T06:37:54.176Z\",\"status\":\"succeeded\",\"job\":{\"command_name\":\"build\",\"target\":\"A\",\"llm\":\"claude\",\"model\":\"haiku\"},\"result\":{\"returncode\":0,\"timed_out\":false}}\\n')\n    f.write(\"not json\\n\")\n    f.write('{\"schema_version\":1,\"execution_id\":\"e2\",\"started_at\":\"2026-10-14T06:37:54.176Z\",\"status\":\"succeeded\",\"job\":{\"command_name\":\"build\",\"target\":\"B\",\"llm\":\"claude\",\"model\":\"haiku\"},\"result\":{\"returncode\":0,\"timed_out\":false}}\\n')\n\ncode_all, out_all, err_all = cli_runner(\"summary\", log)\ncode_f, out_f, err_f = cli_runner(\"summary\", \"--target\", \"A\", log)\nprint(err_all)\nprint(err_f)\nassert code_all == 0, (code_all, out_all, err_all)\nassert code_f == 0, (code_f, out_f, err_f)\n\nskipped_all = out_all.split(\"\\n\")[1].split(\",\")[1]\nskipped_f = out_f.split(\"\\n\")[1].split(\",\")[1]\nassert skipped_all == \"1\", repr(skipped_all)\nassert skipped_f == skipped_all, (skipped_all, skipped_f)\n=== END AC skipped-ignores-target ===\n\n=== AC skipped-ignores-date ===\nIntent: The skipped count is identical with and without date filters.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nimport subprocess\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nlog = \"/tmp/ddkstat-skip-date-fixture.jsonl\"\nwith open(log, \"w\", Report\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Produces the one-row summary report over the filtered records. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-SkippedCount.md, FEATURE-LogFailed.md, FEATURE-LogNumbers.md, FEATURE-FilterTarget.md, FEATURE-FilterDate.md, FEATURE-FormatCSV.md, FEATURE-FormatMinutes.md, FEATURE-FormatCost.md |\n| Provides    | summary report |\n| Consumes    | skipped count, record aggregates, CSV and format helpers |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThe summary report answers the operator's first question: how many records ran, how many succeeded or failed, over what date span, and what tokens, cost, and minutes they used.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Writes one data row under the header `records,skipped,succeeded,failed,first,last,input_tokens,cached_tokens,output_tokens,cost_usd,minutes`.\n- `records` is the count of filtered records; `skipped` is the whole-file skipped count.\n- `succeeded` and `failed` partition the filtered records by status.\n- `first` and `last` are the earliest and latest start dates (first 10 characters of `started_at`) among filtered records, empty when none match.\n- Token columns are the sums of the filtered records' values; absent values count as zero.\n- `cost_usd` is written with `%.4f`; `minutes` is written in tenths of a minute.\n- A zero-match run writes the header and one zero row (`0,<skipped>,0,0,,,0,0,0,0.0000,0.0`).\n- Exit code is 0 whenever the log can be read, including when no record matches.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC summary-header-and-row-shape ===\nIntent: The summary report writes the exact header and one data row with eleven fields.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nlog = \"/tmp/ddkstat-summary-shape.jsonl\"\nwith open(log, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n    f.write('{\"schema_version\":1,\"execution_id\":\"e1\",\"started_at\":\"2026-10-14T06:37:54.176Z\",\"status\":\"succeeded\",\"job\":{\"command_name\":\"build\",\"target\":\"A\",\"llm\":\"claude\",\"model\":\"haiku\"},\"result\":{\"returncode\":0,\"timed_out\":false,\"stats\":{\"cached_input_tokens\":10,\"elapsed_ms\":60000,\"input_tokens\":5,\"output_tokens\":7,\"cost_usd\":0.25,\"model\":\"haiku\"}}}\\n')\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"summary\", log)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert rows[0] == [\"records\", \"skipped\", \"succeeded\", \"failed\", \"first\", \"last\", \"input_tokens\", \"cached_tokens\", \"output_tokens\", \"cost_usd\", \"minutes\"], rows[0]\nassert len(rows) == 2, rows\nassert len(rows[1]) == 11, rows[1]\n=== END AC summary-header-and-row-shape ===\n\n=== AC summary-values-from-fixture ===\nIntent: The summary report aggregates a known fixture into the expected counts, dates, sums, cost, and Filters narrow `records`, `succeeded`, `failed`, tokens, cost, and minutes, but never `skipped`.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CommandsReport.md ===\n# Feature: Commands Report\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Produces one row per command, ordered by calls then command name, plus a TOTAL row. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-FilterTarget.md, FEATURE-FilterDate.md, FEATURE-LogNumbers.md, FEATURE-LogFailed.md, FEATURE-FormatCSV.md, FEATURE-FormatMinutes.md |\n| Provides    | commands report |\n| Consumes    | record aggregates, CSV and format helpers |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThe commands report shows which Drydock commands ran, how often, how many failed, and what tokens and minutes they used.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Header: `command,calls,failed,input_tokens,cached_tokens,output_tokens,minutes`.\n- One row per distinct `job.command_name` among filtered records.\n- Rows are ordered by `calls` descending, then `command` ascending by byte value (AC-006).\n- Each row's `minutes` is written in tenths of a minute (FEATURE-FormatMinutes.md).\n- A final `TOTAL` row aggregates all filtered records.\n- A zero-match run writes the header and one row `TOTAL,0,0,0,0,0,0.0`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC commands-ordering-and-total ===\nIntent: Commands are ordered by calls descending then name ascending, and TOTAL sums the rows.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nlog = \"/tmp/ddkstat-commands-order.jsonl\"\nwith open(log, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n    f.write('{\"schema_version\":1,\"execution_id\":\"e1\",\"started_at\":\"2026-10-14T06:00:00.000Z\",\"status\":\"succeeded\",\"job\":{\"command_name\":\"plan\",\"target\":\"A\",\"llm\":\"claude\",\"model\":\"haiku\"},\"result\":{\"returncode\":0,\"timed_out\":false,\"stats\":{\"cached_input_tokens\":1,\"elapsed_ms\":60000,\"input_tokens\":1,\"output_tokens\":1,\"cost_usd\":0.1,\"model\":\"haiku\"}}}\\n')\n    f.write('{\"schema_version\":1,\"execution_id\":\"e2\",\"started_at\":\"2026-10-14T06:01:00.000Z\",\"status\":\"succeeded\",\"job\":{\"command_name\":\"build\",\"target\":\"A\",\"llm\":\"claude\",\"model\":\"haiku\"},\"result\":{\"returncode\":0,\"timed_out\":false,\"stats\":{\"cached_input_tokens\":1,\"elapsed_ms\":60000,\"input_tokens\":1,\"output_tokens\":1,\"cost_usd\":0.1,\"model\":\"haiku\"}}}\\n')\n    f.write('{\"schema_version\":1,\"execution_id\":\"e3\",\"started_at\":\"2026-10-14T06:02:00.000Z\",\"status\":\"succeeded\",\"job\":{\"command_name\":\"build\",\"target\":\"A\",\"llm\":\"claude\",\"model\":\"haiku\"},\"result\":{\"returncode\":0,\"timed_out\":false,\"stats\":{\"cached_input_tokens\":1,\"elapsed_ms\":60000,\"input_tokens\":1,\"output_tokens\":1,\"cost_usd\":0.1,\"model\":\"haiku\"}}}\\n')\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"commands\", log)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert rows[1][0] == \"build\", rows[1]\nassert rows[2][0] == \"plan\", rows[2]\nassert rows[3][0] == \"TOTAL\", rows[3]\nassert rows[3][1] == \"3\", rows[3]\nassert rows[3][3] == \"3\", rows[3]\n=== END AC commands-ordering-and-total ===\n\n=== AC Purpose\n\nEvery report shares one CSV writer. It writes a header row first, then data rows, comma-separated with `\\n` line endings. Zero-match reports write their header and, where the report defines one, a zero TOTAL row.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The first row written is the report header, exactly as the report defines it.\n- Each subsequent row is written as comma-separated fields, terminated by `\\n`.\n- Line endings are `\\n` only; no `\\r\\n`.\n- A zero-match report writes its header and, where the report has a TOTAL row, a TOTAL row carrying zero values.\n- The writer does not add quoting to plain values. Report\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Produces the one-row summary report over the filtered records. |\n| Depends On  | skipped count.\n- `succeeded` and `failed` partition the filtered records by status.\n- `first` and `last` are the earliest and latest start dates (first 10 characters of `started_at`) among filtered records, empty when none match.\n- Token columns are the sums of the filtered records' values; absent values count as zero.\n- `cost_usd` is written with `%.4f`; `minutes` is written in tenths of a minute.\n- A zero-match run writes the header and one zero row (`0,<skipped>,0,0,,,0,0,0,0.0000,0.0`).\n- Exit code is 0 whenever the log can be read, including when no record matches.\n\n## Report\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Produces one row per command, ordered by calls then command name, plus a TOTAL row. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-FilterTarget.md, FEATURE-FilterDate.md, FEATURE-LogNumbers.md, FEATURE-LogFailed.md, FEATURE-FormatCSV.md, FEATURE-FormatMinutes.md |\n| Provides    | commands report |\n| Consumes    | record aggregates, CSV and format helpers |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThe commands report shows which Drydock commands ran, how often, how many failed, and what tokens and minutes they used.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Header: `command,calls,failed,input_tokens,cached_tokens,output_tokens,minutes`.\n- One row per distinct `job.command_name` among filtered records.\n- Rows are ordered by `calls` descending, then `command` ascending by byte value (AC-006).\n- Each row's `minutes` is written in tenths of a minute (FEATURE-FormatMinutes.md).\n- A final
